Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java

Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a map to make looking up an input by name fast. Also checks for
   * duplicate inputs.
   */
Method code:
428 |   void initInputsByNameMap() {
429 |     inputsByName = new HashMap<String, CompilerInput>();
430 |     for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
431 |       String name = input.getName();
432 |       if (!inputsByName.containsKey(name)) {
433 |         inputsByName.put(name, input);
434 |       } else {
435 |         report(JSError.make(DUPLICATE_EXTERN_INPUT, name));
436 |       }
437 |     }
438 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
439 |       String name = input.getName();
440 |       if (!inputsByName.containsKey(name)) {
441 |         inputsByName.put(name, input);
442 |       } else {
443 |         report(JSError.make(DUPLICATE_INPUT, name));
444 |       }
445 |     }
446 |   }

===== M0013 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ErrorFunctionType.ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ErrorFunctionType.java
Original range: 50-74
Documentation:

Method code:
50 |   ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name) {
51 |     super(
52 |         registry, name, null,
53 |         registry.createArrowType(
54 |             registry.createOptionalParameters(
55 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
56 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
57 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE)),
58 |             null),
59 |         null, null, true, true);
60 | 
61 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Errors have the weird behavior in that they can
62 |     // be called as functions, and they will return instances of themselves.
63 |     // Error('x') instanceof Error => true
64 |     //
65 |     // In user-defined types, we would deal with this case by creating
66 |     // a NamedType with the name "Error" and then resolve it later.
67 |     //
68 |     // For native types, we don't really want the native types to
69 |     // depend on type-resolution. So we just set the return type manually
70 |     // at the end of construction.
71 |     //
72 |     // There's similar logic in JSTypeRegistry for Array and RegExp.
73 |     getInternalArrowType().returnType = getInstanceType();
74 |   }

===== M0021 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SuppressDocWarningsGuard.level(JSError)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SuppressDocWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 49-86
Documentation:

Method code:
49 |   @Override
50 |   public CheckLevel level(JSError error) {
51 |     Node node = error.node;
52 |     if (node != null) {
53 |       for (Node current = node;
54 |            current != null;
55 |            current = current.getParent()) {
56 |         int type = current.getType();
57 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
58 | 
59 |         // We only care about function annotations at the FUNCTION and SCRIPT
60 |         // level. Otherwise, the @suppress annotation has an implicit
61 |         // dependency on the exact structure of our AST, and that seems like
62 |         // a bad idea.
63 |         if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
64 |           info = NodeUtil.getFunctionInfo(current);
65 |         } else if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
66 |           info = current.getJSDocInfo();
67 |         }
68 | 
69 |         if (info != null) {
70 |           for (String suppressor : info.getSuppressions()) {
71 |             WarningsGuard guard = suppressors.get(suppressor);
72 | 
73 |             // Some @suppress tags are for other tools, and
74 |             // may not have a warnings guard.
75 |             if (guard != null) {
76 |               CheckLevel newLevel = guard.level(error);
77 |               if (newLevel != null) {
78 |                 return newLevel;
79 |               }
80 |             }
81 |           }
82 |         }
83 |       }
84 |     }
85 |     return null;
86 |   }

===== M0022 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.isJSDocString(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 280-304
Documentation:

Method code:
280 |   private boolean isJSDocString(int c) {
281 |     switch (c) {
282 |       case '@':
283 |       case '*':
284 |       case ',':
285 |       case '>':
286 |       case ':':
287 |       case '(':
288 |       case ')':
289 |       case '{':
290 |       case '}':
291 |       case '[':
292 |       case ']':
293 |       case '?':
294 |       case '!':
295 |       case '|':
296 |       case '=':
297 |       case EOF_CHAR:
298 |       case '\n':
299 |         return false;
300 | 
301 |       default:
302 |         return !isJSSpace(c);
303 |     }
304 |   }

===== M0024 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ComposeWarningsGuard.addGuard(WarningsGuard)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ComposeWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 56-67
Documentation:

Method code:
56 |   void addGuard(WarningsGuard guard) {
57 |     if (guard instanceof ComposeWarningsGuard) {
58 |       // Reverse the guards, so that they have the same order in the result.
59 |       addGuards(Iterables.reverse(((ComposeWarningsGuard) guard).guards));
60 |     } else {
61 |       int index = Collections.binarySearch(this.guards, guard, guardComparator);
62 |       if (index < 0) {
63 |         index = -index - 1;
64 |       }
65 |       this.guards.add(index, guard);
66 |     }
67 |   }

===== M0033 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CombinedCompilerPass.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CombinedCompilerPass.java
Original range: 156-167
Documentation:

Method code:
156 |   @Override
157 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
158 |     for (CallbackWrapper callback : callbacks) {
159 |       callback.shouldTraverseIfActive(t, n, parent);
160 |     }
161 |     // Note that this method could return false if all callbacks are inactive.
162 |     // This apparent optimization would make this method more expensive
163 |     // in the typical case where not all nodes are inactive. It is
164 |     // very unlikely that many all callbacks would be inactive at the same
165 |     // time (indeed, there are several checking passes that never return false).
166 |     return true;
167 |   }

===== M0038 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 400-416
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
400 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
401 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
402 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
403 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
404 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
405 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
406 |         String inputName = input.getName();
407 | 
408 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
409 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
410 |         // will get an error down the line.
411 |         inputs.add(input);
412 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
413 |       }
414 |     }
415 |     return inputs;
416 |   }

===== M0046 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckGlobalThis.getFunctionJsDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckGlobalThis.java
Original range: 177-193
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets a function's JSDoc information, if it has any. Checks for a few
   * patterns (ellipses show where JSDoc would be):
   * <pre>
   * ... function() {}
   * ... x = function() {};
   * var ... x = function() {};
   * ... var x = function() {};
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
177 |   private JSDocInfo getFunctionJsDocInfo(Node n) {
178 |     JSDocInfo jsDoc = n.getJSDocInfo();
179 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
180 |     if (jsDoc == null) {
181 |       int parentType = parent.getType();
182 |       if (parentType == Token.NAME || parentType == Token.ASSIGN) {
183 |         jsDoc = parent.getJSDocInfo();
184 |         if (jsDoc == null && parentType == Token.NAME) {
185 |           Node gramps = parent.getParent();
186 |           if (gramps.getType() == Token.VAR) {
187 |             jsDoc = gramps.getJSDocInfo();
188 |           }
189 |         }
190 |       }
191 |     }
192 |     return jsDoc;
193 |   }

===== M0062 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 587-613
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
587 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
588 |     ObjectType owner = null;
589 | 
590 |     // Properties can only be defined on object types, so normalize everything
591 |     // to discrete object types.
592 |     if (type instanceof ObjectType) {
593 |       owner = (ObjectType) type;
594 |     } else if (getNativeType(ALL_TYPE).isSubtype(type)) {
595 |       owner = getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.OBJECT_TYPE);
596 |     } else if (type instanceof UnionType) {
597 |       for (JSType alternate : ((UnionType) type).getAlternates()) {
598 |         registerPropertyOnType(propertyName, alternate);
599 |       }
600 |     }
601 | 
602 |     if (owner == null) {
603 |       return;
604 |     }
605 | 
606 |     Set<ObjectType> typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
607 |     if (typeSet == null) {
608 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(
609 |           propertyName, typeSet = new LinkedHashSet<ObjectType>());
610 |     }
611 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
612 |     typeSet.add(owner);
613 |   }

===== M0068 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionParamBuilder.java
Original range: 94-109
Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

===== M0076 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 337-391
Documentation:

Method code:
337 |   protected int getChar() {
338 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
339 |       cursor++;
340 |       --ungetCursor;
341 |       if (charno == -1) {
342 |         charno = getOffset();
343 |       }
344 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
345 |     }
346 | 
347 |     for(;;) {
348 |       int c;
349 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
350 |         hitEOF = true;
351 |         if (charno == -1) {
352 |           charno = getOffset();
353 |         }
354 |         return EOF_CHAR;
355 |       }
356 |       cursor++;
357 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
358 | 
359 | 
360 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
361 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
362 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
363 |           continue;
364 |         }
365 |         lineEndChar = -1;
366 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
367 |         lineno++;
368 |       }
369 | 
370 |       if (c <= 127) {
371 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
372 |           lineEndChar = c;
373 |           c = '\n';
374 |         }
375 |       } else {
376 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
377 |           continue;
378 |         }
379 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
380 |           lineEndChar = c;
381 |           c = '\n';
382 |         }
383 |       }
384 | 
385 |       if (charno == -1) {
386 |         charno = getOffset();
387 |       }
388 | 
389 |       return c;
390 |     }
391 |   }

===== M0087 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 393-437
Documentation:

Method code:
393 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
394 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
395 |       cursor++;
396 |       --ungetCursor;
397 |       if (charno == -1) {
398 |         charno = getOffset();
399 |       }
400 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
401 |     }
402 | 
403 |     for(;;) {
404 |       int c;
405 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
406 |         hitEOF = true;
407 |         if (charno == -1) {
408 |           charno = getOffset();
409 |         }
410 |         return EOF_CHAR;
411 |       }
412 |       cursor++;
413 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
414 | 
415 | 
416 |       if (c <= 127) {
417 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
418 |           lineEndChar = c;
419 |           c = '\n';
420 |         }
421 |       } else {
422 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
423 |           continue;
424 |         }
425 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
426 |           lineEndChar = c;
427 |           c = '\n';
428 |         }
429 |       }
430 | 
431 |       if (charno == -1) {
432 |         charno = getOffset();
433 |       }
434 | 
435 |       return c;
436 |     }
437 |   }

===== M0101 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1434-1436
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1434 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1435 |     return n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION && !isStatement(n);
1436 |   }

===== M0105 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transform(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 204-228
Documentation:

Method code:
204 |   private Node transform(AstNode node) {
205 |     JSDocInfo jsDocInfo = handleJsDoc(node);
206 |     Node irNode = justTransform(node);
207 |     if (jsDocInfo != null) {
208 |       irNode.setJSDocInfo(jsDocInfo);
209 |     }
210 | 
211 |     // If we have a named function, set the position to that of the name.
212 |     if (irNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
213 |         irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno() != -1) {
214 |       irNode.setLineno(irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno());
215 |       irNode.setCharno(irNode.getFirstChild().getCharno());
216 |     } else {
217 |       if (irNode.getLineno() == -1) {
218 |         // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now.  This avoids
219 |         // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
220 |         // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
221 |         int lineno = node.getLineno();
222 |         irNode.setLineno(lineno);
223 |         int charno = position2charno(node.getAbsolutePosition());
224 |         irNode.setCharno(charno);
225 |       }
226 |     }
227 |     return irNode;
228 |   }

/**
   * Builds a {@link JSDocInfo} object based on the populated information and
   * returns it. Once this method is called, the builder can be reused to build
   * another {@link JSDocInfo} object.
   *
   * @param sourceName The source file containing the JSDoc.
   * @return a {@link JSDocInfo} object populated with the values given to this
   *     builder. If no value was populated, this method simply returns
   *     {@code null}

116 |       populateDefaults(built);

118 |       currentInfo = new JSDocInfo(this.parseDocumentation);
119 |       return built;
120 |     } else {
121 |       return null;
122 |     }

===== M0120 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java

Documentation:
/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
775 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
776 |     if (item == null) {
777 |       return null;
778 |     } else if (item.type == propType) {
779 |       return item.next;
780 |     } else {
781 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.next, propType);
782 |       if (result != item.next) {
783 |         return new PropListItem(

785 |       } else {
786 |         return item;
787 |       }
788 |     }

===== M0123 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 427-441
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
427 |   @Override
428 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
429 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
430 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
431 |     if (unknown) {
432 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
433 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
434 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
435 |         unknown = false;
436 |       } else {
437 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
438 |       }
439 |     }
440 |     return unknown;
441 |   }

Method code:
2199 |   static boolean isConstantByConvention(
2200 |       CodingConvention convention, Node node, Node parent) {
2201 |     String name = node.getString();
2202 |     if (parent.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
2203 |         node == parent.getLastChild()) {

2205 |     } else if (isObjectLitKey(node, parent)) {

2208 |       return convention.isConstant(name);
2209 |     }

===== M0131 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 60-77
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
60 |   @Override
61 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
62 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
63 |       return false;
64 |     }
65 | 
66 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
67 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
68 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
69 |     if (pos >= 0) {
70 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
71 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
72 |         return false;
73 |       }
74 |     }
75 | 
76 |     return isConstantKey(name);
77 |   }

===== M0132 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.addToString(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 242-251
Documentation:

Method code:
242 |   private void addToString(int c) {
243 |     int N = stringBufferTop;
244 |     if (N == stringBuffer.length) {
245 |         char[] tmp = new char[stringBuffer.length * 2];
246 |         System.arraycopy(stringBuffer, 0, tmp, 0, N);
247 |         stringBuffer = tmp;
248 |     }
249 |     stringBuffer[N] = (char)c;
250 |     stringBufferTop = N + 1;
251 |   }

===== M0135 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 226-262
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
226 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
227 |     this.options = options;
228 |     if (errorManager == null) {
229 |       if (outStream == null) {
230 |         setErrorManager(
231 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
232 |       } else {
233 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
234 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
235 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
236 |         setErrorManager(printer);
237 |       }
238 |     }
239 | 
240 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
241 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
242 |     guards.add(
243 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
244 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
245 |     WarningsGuard warningsGuard = options.getWarningsGuard();
246 |     if (warningsGuard != null) {
247 |       guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
251 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
252 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
253 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
254 |     // error off.
255 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
256 |         (warningsGuard == null || !warningsGuard.disables(
257 |             DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES))) {
258 |       guards.add(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
259 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
260 |     }
261 |     this.warningsGuard = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
262 |   }

===== M0186 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 294-302
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
294 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
295 |       Node ret = transform(n);
296 |       if (ret.getType() == Token.STRING) {
297 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
298 |       } else if (ret.getType() == Token.NAME) {
299 |         ret.setType(Token.STRING);
300 |       }
301 |       return ret;
302 |     }

===== M0203 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.BasicErrorManager.LeveledJSErrorComparator.compare(ErrorWithLevel,ErrorWithLevel)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/BasicErrorManager.java
Original range: 120-170
Documentation:

Method code:
120 |     public int compare(ErrorWithLevel p1, ErrorWithLevel p2) {
121 |       // null is the smallest value
122 |       if (p2 == null) {
123 |         if (p1 == null) {
124 |           return 0;
125 |         } else {
126 |           return P1_GT_P2;
127 |         }
128 |       }
129 | 
130 |       // check level
131 |       if (p1.level != p2.level) {
132 |         return p2.level.compareTo(p1.level);
133 |       }
134 | 
135 |       // sourceName comparison
136 |       String source1 = p1.error.sourceName;
137 |       String source2 = p2.error.sourceName;
138 |       if (source1 != null && source2 != null) {
139 |         int sourceCompare = source1.compareTo(source2);
140 |         if (sourceCompare != 0) {
141 |           return sourceCompare;
142 |         }
143 |       } else if (source1 == null && source2 != null) {
144 |         return P1_LT_P2;
145 |       } else if (source1 != null && source2 == null) {
146 |         return P1_GT_P2;
147 |       }
148 |       // lineno comparison
149 |       int lineno1 = p1.error.lineNumber;
150 |       int lineno2 = p2.error.lineNumber;
151 |       if (lineno1 != lineno2) {
152 |         return lineno1 - lineno2;
153 |       } else if (lineno1 < 0 && 0 <= lineno2) {
154 |         return P1_LT_P2;
155 |       } else if (0 <= lineno1 && lineno2 < 0) {
156 |         return P1_GT_P2;
157 |       }
158 |       // charno comparison
159 |       int charno1 = p1.error.getCharno();
160 |       int charno2 = p2.error.getCharno();
161 |       if (charno1 != charno2) {
162 |         return charno1 - charno2;
163 |       } else if (charno1 < 0 && 0 <= charno2) {
164 |         return P1_LT_P2;
165 |       } else if (0 <= charno1 && charno2 < 0) {
166 |         return P1_GT_P2;
167 |       }
168 |       // description
169 |       return p1.error.description.compareTo(p2.error.description);
170 |     }

===== M0208 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 420-446
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
420 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
421 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
422 | 
423 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
424 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
425 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
426 |       return 0;
427 |     }
428 | 
429 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
430 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
431 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than extraTracingStatistics.size() because
432 |       // a new statistic may have been added
433 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
434 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
435 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
436 |       }
437 |     }
438 | 
439 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
440 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
441 |       return 0;
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
445 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
446 |   }

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
407 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
408 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
409 |          current != null;
410 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
411 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
412 |         return true;
413 |       }
414 |     }
415 |     return false;
416 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 59-195
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
59 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
60 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
61 |     int c;
62 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
63 |     for (;;) {
64 |       // eat white spaces
65 |       for (;;) {
66 |         charno = -1;
67 |         c = getChar();
68 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
69 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
70 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
71 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
72 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
73 |           break;
74 |         }
75 |       }
76 | 
77 |       switch (c) {
78 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
79 |         case '@':
80 |           do {
81 |             c = getChar();
82 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
83 |               addToString(c);
84 |             } else {
85 |               ungetChar(c);
86 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
87 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
88 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
89 |             }
90 |           } while (true);
91 | 
92 |         case '*':
93 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
94 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
95 |           } else {
96 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
97 |           }
98 | 
99 |         case ',':
100 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
101 | 
102 |         case '>':
103 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
104 | 
105 |         case '(':
106 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
107 | 
108 |         case ')':
109 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
110 | 
111 |         case '{':
112 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
113 | 
114 |         case '}':
115 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
116 | 
117 |         case '[':
118 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
119 | 
120 |         case ']':
121 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
122 | 
123 |         case '?':
124 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
125 | 
126 |         case '!':
127 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
128 | 
129 |         case ':':
130 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
131 | 
132 |         case '=':
133 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
134 | 
135 |         case '|':
136 |           matchChar('|');
137 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
138 | 
139 |         case '.':
140 |           c = getChar();
141 |           if (c == '<') {
142 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
143 |           } else {
144 |             if (c == '.') {
145 |               c = getChar();
146 |               if (c == '.') {
147 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
148 |               } else {
149 |                 addToString('.');
150 |               }
151 |             }
152 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
153 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
154 |             c = '.';
155 |           }
156 |           // fall through
157 | 
158 |         default: {
159 |           // recognize a jsdoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
160 |           // a non-jsdoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
161 |           int c1 = c;
162 |           addToString(c);
163 |           int c2 = getChar();
164 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
165 |             ungetChar(c2);
166 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
167 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
168 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
169 |           } else {
170 |             do {
171 |               c1 = c2;
172 |               c2 = getChar();
173 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
174 |                 ungetChar(c2);
175 |                 ungetChar(c1);
176 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
177 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
178 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
179 |               } else {
180 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
181 |                   addToString(c1);
182 |                 } else {
183 |                   ungetChar(c2);
184 |                   addToString(c1);
185 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
186 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
187 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
188 |                 }
189 |               }
190 |             } while (true);
191 |           }
192 |         }
193 |       }
194 |     }
195 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 87-142
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
87 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
88 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
89 |     // just bail out early here.
90 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
91 |       return this;
92 |     }
93 | 
94 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
95 | 
96 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
97 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
98 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
99 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
100 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
101 |     }
102 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
103 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
104 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
105 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
106 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
107 |         }
108 |       } else {
109 |         if (alternates.size() > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
110 |           return this;
111 |         }
112 | 
113 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
114 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
115 |         // one another.
116 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
117 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
118 |           JSType current = it.next();
119 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
120 |               current.isUnknownType()) {
121 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
122 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
123 |               return this;
124 |             }
125 |           } else {
126 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
127 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
128 |               return this;
129 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
130 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
131 |               it.remove();
132 |             }
133 |           }
134 |         }
135 |         alternates.add(alternate);
136 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
137 |       }
138 |     } else {
139 |       result = null;
140 |     }
141 |     return this;
142 |   }

===== M0219 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckGlobalThis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckGlobalThis.java
Original range: 82-146
Documentation:
/**
   * Since this pass reports errors only when a global {@code this} keyword
   * is encountered, there is no reason to traverse non global contexts.
   */
Method code:
82 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
83 | 
84 |     if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
85 |       // Don't traverse functions that are constructors or have the @this
86 |       // or @override annotation.
87 |       JSDocInfo jsDoc = getFunctionJsDocInfo(n);
88 |       if (jsDoc != null &&
89 |           (jsDoc.isConstructor() ||
90 |            jsDoc.isInterface() ||
91 |            jsDoc.hasThisType() ||
92 |            jsDoc.isOverride())) {
93 |         return false;
94 |       }
95 | 
96 |       // Don't traverse functions unless they would normally
97 |       // be able to have a @this annotation associated with them. e.g.,
98 |       // var a = function() { }; // or
99 |       // function a() {} // or
100 |       // a.x = function() {}; // or
101 |       // var a = {x: function() {}};
102 |       int pType = parent.getType();
103 |       if (!(pType == Token.BLOCK ||
104 |             pType == Token.SCRIPT ||
105 |             pType == Token.NAME ||
106 |             pType == Token.ASSIGN ||
107 | 
108 |             // object literal keys
109 |             pType == Token.STRING ||
110 |             pType == Token.NUMBER)) {
111 |         return false;
112 |       }
113 | 
114 |       // Don't traverse functions that are getting lent to a prototype.
115 |     }
116 | 
117 |     if (parent != null && parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
118 |       Node lhs = parent.getFirstChild();
119 |       Node rhs = lhs.getNext();
120 | 
121 |       if (n == lhs) {
122 |         // Always traverse the left side of the assignment. To handle
123 |         // nested assignments properly (e.g., (a = this).property = c;),
124 |         // assignLhsChild should not be overridden.
125 |         if (assignLhsChild == null) {
126 |           assignLhsChild = lhs;
127 |         }
128 |       } else {
129 |         // Only traverse the right side if it's not an assignment to a prototype
130 |         // property or subproperty.
131 |         if (NodeUtil.isGet(lhs)) {
132 |           if (lhs.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
133 |               lhs.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
134 |             return false;
135 |           }
136 |           Node llhs = lhs.getFirstChild();
137 |           if (llhs.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
138 |               llhs.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
139 |             return false;
140 |           }
141 |         }
142 |       }
143 |     }
144 | 
145 |     return true;
146 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)

Original range: 361-376
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
361 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
362 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
363 |       Set<String> directives = null;
364 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
365 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
366 |         if (directives == null) {
367 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
368 |         } else {
369 |           directives.add(directive);
370 |         }
371 |       }
372 | 
373 |       if (directives != null) {
374 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
375 |       }
376 |     }

===== M0227 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 406-450
Documentation:

Method code:
406 |   @Override
407 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
408 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
409 |       return true;
410 |     }
411 | 
412 |     // Union types
413 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
414 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
415 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
416 |       return false;
417 |     }
418 | 
419 |     // record types
420 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
421 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
422 |     }
423 | 
424 |     // Interfaces
425 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
426 |     // to the interface instance.
427 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
428 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
429 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
430 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
431 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
432 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
433 |           return true;
434 |         }
435 |       }
436 |     }
437 | 
438 |     // other prototype based objects
439 |     if (that != null) {
440 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
441 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
442 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
443 |         // to avoid guessing.
444 |         return true;
445 |       }
446 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     return false;
450 |   }

===== M0254 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1028-1165
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1028 |   Node parseInputs() {
1029 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1030 | 
1031 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1032 |     // individual file parse trees.
1033 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1034 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1035 |     }
1036 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1037 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1038 |     }
1039 | 
1040 |     // Parse main js sources.
1041 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1042 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1043 | 
1044 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1045 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1046 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1047 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1048 |     }
1049 | 
1050 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1051 | 
1052 |     try {
1053 |       // Parse externs sources.
1054 |       externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1055 |       externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1056 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1057 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1058 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1059 |           return null;
1060 |         }
1061 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1062 |       }
1063 | 
1064 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1065 |       if (options.manageClosureDependencies) {
1066 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1067 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1068 | 
1069 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1070 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1071 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1072 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1073 |           }
1074 |         }
1075 | 
1076 |         try {
1077 |           inputs =
1078 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1079 |               .manageDependencies(
1080 |                   options.manageClosureDependenciesEntryPoints, inputs);
1081 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1082 |           report(JSError.make(
1083 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1084 |           return null;
1085 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1086 |           report(JSError.make(
1087 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1088 |           return null;
1089 |         }
1090 |       }
1091 | 
1092 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1093 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1094 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1095 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1096 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1097 |           return null;
1098 |         }
1099 | 
1100 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1101 |         if (n == null) {
1102 |           continue;
1103 |         }
1104 | 
1105 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1106 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1107 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1108 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1109 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1110 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1111 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1112 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1113 | 
1114 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1115 | 
1116 |             externs.add(input);
1117 |             staleInputs = true;
1118 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1119 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1120 |             staleInputs = true;
1121 |           }
1122 |         }
1123 |       }
1124 | 
1125 |       if (staleInputs) {
1126 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1127 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1128 |       }
1129 | 
1130 |       // Build the AST.
1131 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1132 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1133 |         if (n == null) {
1134 |           continue;
1135 |         }
1136 | 
1137 |         if (devMode) {
1138 |           runSanityCheck();
1139 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1140 |             return null;
1141 |           }
1142 |         }
1143 | 
1144 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1145 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1146 | 
1147 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1148 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1149 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1150 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1151 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1152 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1153 |         }
1154 | 
1155 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1156 |       }
1157 | 
1158 |       externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1159 |       externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1160 | 
1161 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1162 |     } finally {
1163 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1164 |     }
1165 |   }

   *

/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.

   */
Method code:
80 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
81 |                            String sourceString,
82 |                            Config config,
83 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
84 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
85 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
86 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
87 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
88 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
89 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);

100 |     try {
101 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
102 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
103 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
104 |     } finally {
105 |       Context.exit();
106 |     }
107 |     Node root = null;
108 |     if (astRoot != null) {
109 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
110 |           astRoot, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
111 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
112 |     }
113 |     return root;
114 |   }

===== M0266 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 502-552
Documentation:

Method code:
502 |    @Override
503 |   Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
504 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
505 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
506 |       if (name == null) {
507 |         name = new Name();
508 |         name.setIdentifier("");
509 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
510 |       }
511 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
512 |       Node newName = transform(name);
513 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
514 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
515 |         // declaration.
516 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
517 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
518 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
519 |         // same line as the function keyword.
520 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
521 |             functionNode.getLp();
522 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
523 |       }
524 | 
525 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
526 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.LP);
527 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
528 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
529 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
530 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
531 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
532 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
533 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
534 |       if (fnName != null) {
535 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
536 |       } else {
537 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
538 |       }
539 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
540 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
541 | 
542 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
543 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
544 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
545 |       }
546 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
547 | 
548 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
549 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
550 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
551 |      return node;
552 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 437-465
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
437 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
438 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
439 |     int type = n.getType();
440 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
441 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     curNode = n;
445 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
446 | 
447 |     switch (type) {
448 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
449 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
450 |         break;
451 | 
452 |       default:
453 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
454 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
455 |           // would no longer point to the true next
456 |           Node next = child.getNext();
457 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
458 |           child = next;
459 |         }
460 |         break;
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     curNode = n;
464 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
465 |   }

===== M0296 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parse()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 137-873
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
137 |   boolean parse() {
138 |     int lineno;
139 |     int charno;
140 | 
141 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.
142 |     JSTypeExpression type;
143 | 
144 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
145 |     skipEOLs();
146 | 
147 |     JsDocToken token = next();
148 | 
149 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
150 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
151 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
152 |       token = blockInfo.token;
153 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
154 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
155 |       }
156 |     } else {
157 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
158 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
159 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
160 |         // what it was.
161 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");
162 |       }
163 |     }
164 | 
165 |     // Parse the actual JsDoc.
166 |     retry: for (;;) {
167 |       switch (token) {
168 |         case ANNOTATION:
169 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
170 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
171 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
172 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
173 | 
174 |             String annotationName = stream.getString();
175 |             Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
176 |             if (annotation == null) {
177 |               parser.addWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
178 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
179 |             } else {
180 |               // Mark the beginning of the annotation.
181 |               jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);
182 | 
183 |               switch (annotation) {
184 |                 case AUTHOR:
185 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
186 |                     ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
187 |                     String author = authorInfo.string;
188 | 
189 |                     if (author.length() == 0) {
190 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.authormissing",
191 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
192 |                     } else {
193 |                       jsdocBuilder.addAuthor(author);
194 |                     }
195 |                     token = authorInfo.token;
196 |                   } else {
197 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
198 |                   }
199 |                   continue retry;
200 | 
201 |                 case CONSTANT:
202 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
203 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
204 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
205 |                   }
206 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
207 |                   continue retry;
208 | 
209 |                 case CONSTRUCTOR:
210 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstructor()) {
211 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isInterfaceRecorded()) {
212 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
213 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
214 |                     } else {
215 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
216 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
217 |                     }
218 |                   }
219 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
220 |                   continue retry;
221 | 
222 |                 case DEPRECATED:
223 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecated()) {
224 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.deprecated",
225 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
226 |                   }
227 | 
228 |                   // Find the reason/description, if any.
229 |                   ExtractionInfo reasonInfo =
230 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
231 | 
232 |                   String reason = reasonInfo.string;
233 | 
234 |                   if (reason.length() > 0) {
235 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecationReason(reason);
236 |                   }
237 | 
238 |                   token = reasonInfo.token;
239 |                   continue retry;
240 | 
241 |                 case INTERFACE:
242 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordInterface()) {
243 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isConstructorRecorded()) {
244 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
245 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
246 |                     } else {
247 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
248 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
249 |                     }
250 |                   }
251 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
252 |                   continue retry;
253 | 
254 |                 case DESC:
255 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.isDescriptionRecorded()) {
256 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.desc.extra",
257 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
258 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
259 |                     continue retry;
260 |                   } else {
261 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
262 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
263 | 
264 |                     String description = descriptionInfo.string;
265 | 
266 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDescription(description);
267 |                     token = descriptionInfo.token;
268 |                     continue retry;
269 |                   }
270 | 
271 |                 case FILE_OVERVIEW:
272 |                   String fileOverview = "";
273 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
274 |                     ExtractionInfo fileOverviewInfo =
275 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
276 |                             WhitespaceOption.TRIM);
277 | 
278 |                     fileOverview = fileOverviewInfo.string;
279 | 
280 |                     token = fileOverviewInfo.token;
281 |                   } else {
282 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
283 |                   }
284 | 
285 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordFileOverview(fileOverview) ||
286 |                       fileOverviewJSDocInfo != null) {
287 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.fileoverview.extra",
288 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
289 |                   }
290 |                   continue retry;
291 | 
292 |                 case LICENSE:
293 |                 case PRESERVE:
294 |                   ExtractionInfo preserveInfo =
295 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
296 |                                                    WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE);
297 | 
298 |                   String preserve = preserveInfo.string;
299 | 
300 |                   if (preserve.length() > 0) {
301 |                     if (fileLevelJsDocBuilder != null) {
302 |                       fileLevelJsDocBuilder.append(preserve);
303 |                     }
304 |                   }
305 | 
306 |                   token = preserveInfo.token;
307 |                   continue retry;
308 | 
309 |                 case ENUM:
310 |                   token = next();
311 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
312 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
313 | 
314 |                   type = null;
315 |                   if (token != JsDocToken.EOL && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
316 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
317 |                         parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));
318 |                   }
319 | 
320 |                   if (type == null) {
321 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(newStringNode("number"));
322 |                   }
323 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordEnumParameterType(type)) {
324 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
325 |                   }
326 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
327 |                   continue retry;
328 |

333 |                   }
334 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();

340 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
341 |                   }

355 |                   skipEOLs();

357 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
358 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();

360 | 
361 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
362 |                     token = next();
363 |                     matchingRc = true;
364 |                   }

366 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
367 |                     Node typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNameNode(
368 |                         token, lineno, charno, matchingRc);

373 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
374 |                     if (typeNode != null && !matchingRc) {
375 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
376 |                     }

378 | 
379 |                     if (annotation == Annotation.EXTENDS) {
380 |                       if (!jsdocBuilder.recordBaseType(type)) {
381 |                         parser.addWarning(
382 |                             "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
383 |                       }
384 |                     } else {
385 |                       Preconditions.checkState(
386 |                           annotation == Annotation.IMPLEMENTS);
387 |                       if (!jsdocBuilder.recordImplementedInterface(type)) {
388 |                         parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.implements.duplicate",
389 |                             lineno, charno);
390 |                       }
391 |                     }
392 |                     token = next();

394 |                       if (token != JsDocToken.RC) {
395 |                         parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc",
396 |                             stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
397 |                       }
398 |                     } else if (token != JsDocToken.EOL &&
399 |                         token != JsDocToken.EOF && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
400 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.end.annotation.expected",
401 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
402 |                     }
403 |                   } else {
404 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.no.type.name", lineno, charno);
405 |                   }
406 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
407 |                   continue retry;
408 | 
409 |                 case HIDDEN:
410 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordHiddenness()) {
411 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.hidden",
412 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
413 |                   }
414 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
415 |                   continue retry;
416 | 
417 |                 case LENDS:
418 |                   skipEOLs();

420 |                   matchingRc = false;
421 |                   if (match(JsDocToken.LC)) {
422 |                     token = next();
423 |                     matchingRc = true;
424 |                   }

426 |                   if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
427 |                     token = next();
428 |                     if (!jsdocBuilder.recordLends(stream.getString())) {
429 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.lends.incompatible",
430 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
431 |                     }
432 |                   } else {
433 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.lends.missing",
434 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
435 |                   }
436 | 
437 |                   if (matchingRc && !match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
438 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc",
439 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
440 |                   }

442 |                   continue retry;

448 |                   token = meaningInfo.token;

452 |                   }

466 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
467 |                   }

469 |                   continue retry;

472 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoTypeCheck()) {
473 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.nocheck",
474 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
475 |                   }

477 |                   continue retry;

479 |                 case NOT_IMPLEMENTED:

481 |                   continue retry;

483 |                 case INHERIT_DOC:

487 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
488 |                   }

490 |                   continue retry;

492 |                 case THROWS:
493 |                   skipEOLs();
494 |                   token = next();
495 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
496 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
497 |                   type = null;
498 | 
499 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
500 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
501 |                         parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));
502 | 
503 |                     if (type == null) {
504 |                       // parsing error reported during recursive descent
505 |                       // recovering parsing
506 |                       token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
507 |                       continue retry;
508 |                     }
509 |                   }
510 | 
511 |                   // *Update* the token to that after the type annotation.
512 |                   token = current();

514 |                   // Save the throw type.
515 |                   jsdocBuilder.recordThrowType(type);
516 | 
517 |                   // Find the throw's description (if applicable).
518 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
519 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
520 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
521 | 
522 |                     String description = descriptionInfo.string;
523 | 
524 |                     if (description.length() > 0) {
525 |                       jsdocBuilder.recordThrowDescription(type, description);
526 |                     }
527 | 
528 |                     token = descriptionInfo.token;
529 |                   } else {
530 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
531 |                   }
532 |                   continue retry;
533 | 
534 |                 case PARAM:
535 |                   skipEOLs();
536 |                   token = next();
537 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
538 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
539 |                   type = null;
540 | 
541 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
542 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
543 |                         parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(token));
544 | 
545 |                     if (type == null) {
546 |                       // parsing error reported during recursive descent
547 |                       // recovering parsing
548 |                       token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
549 |                       continue retry;
550 |                     }
551 |                     skipEOLs();
552 |                     token = next();
553 |                     lineno = stream.getLineno();
554 |                     charno = stream.getCharno();
555 |                   }
556 | 
557 |                   String name = null;
558 |                   boolean isBracketedParam = JsDocToken.LB == token;
559 |                   if (isBracketedParam) {
560 |                     token = next();
561 |                   }
562 | 
563 |                   if (JsDocToken.STRING != token) {
564 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.missing.variable.name",
565 |                         lineno, charno);
566 |                   } else {
567 |                     name = stream.getString();
568 | 
569 |                     if (isBracketedParam) {
570 |                       token = next();
571 | 
572 |                       // Throw out JsDocToolkit's "default" parameter annotation.
573 |                       // It makes no sense under our type system.
574 |                       if (JsDocToken.EQUALS == token) {
575 |                         token = next();
576 |                         if (JsDocToken.STRING == token) {
577 |                           token = next();
578 |                         }
579 |                       }
580 | 
581 |                       if (JsDocToken.RB != token) {
582 |                         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rb");
583 |                       } else if (type != null) {
584 |                         // Make the type expression optional, if it isn't
585 |                         // already.
586 |                         type = JSTypeExpression.makeOptionalArg(type);
587 |                       }
588 |                     }
589 | 
590 |                     // If the param name has a DOT in it, just throw it out
591 |                     // quietly. We do not handle the JsDocToolkit method
592 |                     // for handling properties of params.
593 |                     if (name.indexOf('.') > -1) {
594 |                       name = null;
595 |                     } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordParameter(name, type)) {
596 |                       if (jsdocBuilder.hasParameter(name)) {
597 |                         parser.addWarning("msg.dup.variable.name", name,
598 |                             lineno, charno);
599 |                       } else {
600 |                         parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", name,
601 |                             lineno, charno);
602 |                       }
603 |                     }
604 |                   }
605 | 
606 |                   if (name == null) {
607 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
608 |                     continue retry;
609 |                   }
610 | 
611 |                   jsdocBuilder.markName(name, lineno, charno);
612 | 
613 |                   // Find the parameter's description (if applicable).
614 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
615 |                     ExtractionInfo paramDescriptionInfo =
616 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
617 | 
618 |                     String paramDescription = paramDescriptionInfo.string;
619 | 
620 |                     if (paramDescription.length() > 0) {
621 |                       jsdocBuilder.recordParameterDescription(name,
622 |                           paramDescription);
623 |                     }
624 | 
625 |                     token = paramDescriptionInfo.token;
626 |                   } else {
627 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
628 |                   }
629 |                   continue retry;
630 | 
631 |                 case PRESERVE_TRY:
632 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordPreserveTry()) {
633 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.preservertry",
634 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
635 |                   }
636 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
637 |                   continue retry;
638 | 
639 |                 case PRIVATE:
640 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PRIVATE)) {
641 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.private",
642 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
643 |                   }
644 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
645 |                   continue retry;
646 | 
647 |                 case PROTECTED:
648 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PROTECTED)) {
649 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.protected",
650 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
651 |                   }
652 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
653 |                   continue retry;
654 | 
655 |                 case PUBLIC:
656 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PUBLIC)) {
657 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.public",
658 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
659 |                   }
660 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
661 |                   continue retry;
662 | 
663 |                 case NO_SHADOW:
664 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoShadow()) {
665 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.noshadow",
666 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
667 |                   }
668 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
669 |                   continue retry;
670 | 
671 |                 case NO_SIDE_EFFECTS:
672 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoSideEffects()) {
673 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.nosideeffects",
674 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
675 |                   }
676 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
677 |                   continue retry;
678 | 
679 |                 case MODIFIES:
680 |                   token = parseModifiesTag(next());
681 |                   continue retry;
682 | 
683 |                 case IMPLICIT_CAST:
684 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordImplicitCast()) {
685 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.implicitcast",
686 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
687 |                   }

689 |                   continue retry;
690 | 
691 |                 case SEE:

693 |                     ExtractionInfo referenceInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
694 |                     String reference = referenceInfo.string;
695 | 
696 |                     if (reference.length() == 0) {
697 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.seemissing",

715 |                   String templateTypeName = templateInfo.string;
716 | 
717 |                   if (templateTypeName.length() == 0) {
718 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.templatemissing",
719 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
720 |                   } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTemplateTypeName(
721 |                       templateTypeName)) {
722 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.template.at.most.once",
723 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
724 |                   }

727 |                   continue retry;
728 |

731 |                   String version = versionInfo.string;
732 |

734 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.versionmissing",
735 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
736 |                   } else {

738 |                        parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.extraversion",
739 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
740 |                     }
