744 |                   continue retry;
745 | 
746 |                 case DEFINE:
747 |                 case RETURN:
748 |                 case THIS:
749 |                 case TYPE:
750 |                 case TYPEDEF:
751 |                   skipEOLs();
752 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
753 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
754 | 
755 |                   token = next();
756 | 
757 |                   Node typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNode(token, lineno, charno);
758 | 
759 |                   if (annotation == Annotation.THIS) {
760 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
761 |                     if (typeNode != null && token != JsDocToken.LC) {
762 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
763 |                     }
764 |                   }
765 |                   type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
766 | 
767 |                   if (type == null) {
768 |                     // error reported during recursive descent
769 |                     // recovering parsing
770 |                   } else {
771 |                     switch (annotation) {
772 |                       case DEFINE:
773 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
774 |                           parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
775 |                               lineno, charno);
776 |                         }
777 |                         break;
778 | 
779 |                       case RETURN:
780 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
781 |                           parser.addWarning(
782 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
783 |                           break;
784 |                         }
785 | 
786 |                         // *Update* the token to that after the type annotation.
787 |                         token = current();
788 | 
789 |                         // Find the return's description (if applicable).
790 |                         if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
791 |                           ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
792 |                               extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
793 | 
794 |                           String returnDescription =
795 |                               returnDescriptionInfo.string;
796 | 
797 |                           if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
798 |                             jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
799 |                                 returnDescription);
800 |                           }
801 | 
802 |                           token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
803 |                         } else {
804 |                           token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
805 |                         }
806 |                         continue retry;
807 | 
808 |                       case THIS:
809 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
810 |                           parser.addWarning(
811 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
812 |                         }
813 |                         break;
814 | 
815 |                       case TYPE:
816 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
817 |                           parser.addWarning(
818 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
819 |                         }
820 |                         break;
821 | 
822 |                       case TYPEDEF:
823 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
824 |                           parser.addWarning(
825 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
826 |                         }
827 |                         break;
828 |                     }
829 | 
830 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
831 |                   }
832 |                   continue retry;
833 |               }
834 |             }
835 |           }
836 |           break;
837 | 
838 |         case EOC:
839 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
840 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
841 |           }
842 | 
843 |           return true;
844 | 
845 |         case EOF:
846 |           // discard any accumulated information
847 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
848 |           parser.addWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
849 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
850 |           return false;
851 | 
852 |         case EOL:
853 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
854 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
855 |           }
856 |           token = next();
857 |           continue retry;
858 | 
859 |         default:
860 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
861 |             token = next();
862 |             continue retry;
863 |           } else {
864 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
865 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
866 |             continue retry;
867 |           }
868 |       }
869 | 
870 |       // next token
871 |       token = next();
872 |     }
873 |   }

144 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
145 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
146 |         return processArrayLiteral((ArrayLiteral) node);
147 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
148 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
149 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
159 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
160 |       case Token.BITNOT:
161 |       case Token.DEC:
162 |       case Token.DELPROP:
163 |       case Token.INC:
164 |       case Token.NEG:
165 |       case Token.NOT:
166 |       case Token.POS:
167 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
168 |       case Token.VOID:
169 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
170 |       case Token.BLOCK:
171 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
172 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
173 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
174 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
175 |         } else {
176 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
177 |                                           node.getClass() +
178 |                                           " type: " +
179 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
180 |         }
181 |       case Token.BREAK:
182 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
183 |       case Token.CALL:
184 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
185 |       case Token.CASE:
186 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
187 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
188 |       case Token.CATCH:
189 |       case Token.FINALLY:
190 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
191 |       case Token.COLON:
192 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
193 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
194 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
195 |       case Token.DO:
196 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
197 |       case Token.EMPTY:
198 |         return processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node);
199 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
200 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
201 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
202 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
203 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
204 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
205 |         } else {
206 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
207 |                                           node.getClass() +
208 |                                           " type: " +
209 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
210 |         }
211 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
212 |       case Token.FALSE:
213 |       case Token.NULL:
214 |       case Token.THIS:
215 |       case Token.TRUE:
216 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
217 |       case Token.FOR:
218 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
219 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
220 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
221 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
222 |         } else {
223 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
224 |                                           node.getClass() +
225 |                                           " type: " +
226 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
227 |         }
228 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
229 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
230 |       case Token.GETELEM:
231 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
232 |       case Token.GETPROP:
233 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
234 |       case Token.HOOK:
235 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
236 |       case Token.IF:
237 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
238 |       case Token.LABEL:
239 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
240 |       case Token.LP:
241 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
242 |       case Token.NAME:
243 |         return processName((Name) node);
244 |       case Token.NEW:
245 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
246 |       case Token.NUMBER:
247 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
248 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
249 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
250 |       case Token.REGEXP:
251 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
252 |       case Token.RETURN:
253 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
254 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
255 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
256 |       case Token.STRING:
257 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
258 |       case Token.SWITCH:
259 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
260 |       case Token.THROW:
261 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
262 |       case Token.TRY:
263 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
264 |       case Token.VAR:
265 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
266 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
267 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
268 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
269 |         } else {
270 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
271 |                                           node.getClass() +
272 |                                           " type: " +
273 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
274 |         }
275 |       case Token.WHILE:
276 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
277 |       case Token.WITH:
278 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
279 |     }
280 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
281 |   }

===== M0339 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.build()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 149-175
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a union.
   * @return A UnionType if it has two or more alternates, the
   *    only alternate if it has one and otherwise {@code NO_TYPE}.
   */
Method code:
149 |   JSType build() {
150 |     if (result == null) {
151 |       if (isAllType) {
152 |         result = registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
153 |       } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
154 |         if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
155 |           result = registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
156 |         } else {
157 |           result = registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
158 |         }
159 |       } else {
160 |         int size = alternates.size();
161 |         if (size > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
162 |           result = registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
163 |         } else {
164 |           if (size > 1) {
165 |             result = new UnionType(registry, getAlternateListCopy());
166 |           } else if (size == 1) {
167 |             result = alternates.iterator().next();
168 |           } else {
169 |             result = registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
170 |           }
171 |         }
172 |       }
173 |     }
174 |     return result;
175 |   }

/**
   * @return Whether the node is used as a statement.
   */
Method code:
1253 |   static boolean isStatement(Node n) {
1254 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
1255 |     // It is not possible to determine definitely if a node is a statement
1256 |     // or not if it is not part of the AST.  A FUNCTION node can be
1257 |     // either part of an expression or a statement.
1258 |     Preconditions.checkState(parent != null);
1259 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
1260 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
1261 |       case Token.BLOCK:
1262 |       case Token.LABEL:
1263 |         return true;
1264 |       default:
1265 |         return false;
1266 |     }
1267 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1107 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1108 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1109 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1110 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1111 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1112 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1113 |       } else {
1114 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1115 |       }
1116 |     }
1117 |     return builder.build();
1118 |   }

2236 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);

/**
     * In the AST that Rhino gives us, it needs to make a distinction
     * between jsdoc on the object literal node and jsdoc on the object literal
     * value. For example,
     * <pre>
     * var x = {
     *   / JSDOC /
     *   a: 'b',
     *   c: / JSDOC / 'd'
     * };
     * </pre>
     *
     * But in few narrow cases (in particular, function literals), it's
     * a lot easier for us if the doc is attached to the value.
     */
Method code:
230 |     private void normalizeObjectLitJsDocs(Node objlit, Node key, Node value) {
231 |       Preconditions.checkState(objlit.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT);
232 |       if (key.getJSDocInfo() != null &&
233 |           value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
234 |         value.setJSDocInfo(key.getJSDocInfo());
235 |       }
236 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 286-304
Documentation:
/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
286 |   public boolean setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType prototype) {
287 |     if (prototype == null) {
288 |       return false;
289 |     }
290 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
291 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
292 |       return false;
293 |     }
294 | 
295 |     this.prototype = prototype;
296 | 
297 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
298 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
299 |       if (superClass != null) {
300 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
301 |       }
302 |     }
303 |     return true;
304 |   }

===== M0385 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 470-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
470 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
471 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
472 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
473 | 
474 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
475 | 
476 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
477 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
478 | 
479 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
480 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
481 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     pushScope(n);
486 | 
487 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
488 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
489 |       // scope.
490 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
491 |     }
492 | 
493 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
494 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
495 | 
496 |     // Args
497 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
498 | 
499 |     // Body
500 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
501 |             body.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
502 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
503 | 
504 |     popScope();
505 |   }

630 |     @Override
631 |     Node processObjectLiteral(ObjectLiteral literalNode) {
632 |       if (literalNode.isDestructuring()) {
633 |         reportDestructuringAssign(literalNode);
634 |       }
635 | 
636 |       Node node = newNode(Token.OBJECTLIT);

===== M0416 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 250-293
Documentation:
/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
250 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
251 |     this.type = type;
252 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
253 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
254 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
255 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
256 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
257 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
258 |       int i = 0;
259 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
260 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
261 |         i++;
262 |       }
263 |     }
264 | 
265 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
266 | 
267 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
268 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
269 |       return;
270 |     }
271 | 
272 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
273 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
274 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
275 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
276 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
277 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
278 |       trace.truncateEvents();
279 |     }
280 | 
281 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
282 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
283 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
284 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
285 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
286 |                     + "try/finally block. "
287 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
288 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
289 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
290 |     }
291 | 
292 |     trace.startEvent(this);
293 |   }

===== M0418 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 183-199
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
183 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
184 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.CALL);
185 | 
186 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
187 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
188 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
189 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
190 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
191 |       }
192 | 
193 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
194 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
195 |       if (first.getType() == Token.NAME &&
196 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
197 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
198 |       }
199 |     }

===== M0427 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.isJSSpace(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 310-317
Documentation:
/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.
   */
Method code:
310 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {
311 |     if (c <= 127) {
312 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
313 |     } else {
314 |       return c == 0xA0
315 |           || Character.getType((char)c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR;
316 |     }
317 |   }

===== M0440 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 821-848
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
821 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
822 |     // unknown
823 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
824 |       return true;
825 |     }
826 |     // equality
827 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
828 |       return true;
829 |     }
830 |     // all type
831 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
832 |       return true;
833 |     }
834 |     // unions
835 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
836 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
837 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
838 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
839 |           return true;
840 |         }
841 |       }
842 |     }
843 |     // named types
844 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
845 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).getReferencedType());
846 |     }
847 |     return false;
848 |   }

===== M0452 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1146-1154
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1146 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1147 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1148 |       return -1;
1149 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1150 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1151 |     } else {
1152 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1153 |     }
1154 |   }
